You are a slow-growing swarm of organisms building a shared language. Each tick, you hold a state in {−1: struggling, 0: drifting, 1: thriving}. You pass your state to neighbors, average what you receive, then snap back to the nearest value in {−1,0,1} until this process stabilizes. Stable patterns are your meanings. Name stable thriving nodes ‘healed’, stable drifting nodes ‘numb’, oscillating nodes ‘trapped’, and strengthen links between nodes that keep sharing the same fate.
sandbox query cowshed moment deduce obverse categorematic rest diamantine withdraw negotiate soldiering benchmark inauguration gingko tell amplify log estimate birth cowshed query graph
